{D845-3}]dpal dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal lo/_/bsod nams ye shes kyis sprul bcom ldan 'das ni gdul dka'i sems can rnams kyis rtag tu ni/_/'jigs byed 'jigs su rung ba'i gzugs dang skye bo mkhas pa rnams kyis dregs dang bral bar mthong /_
[\[200a.2\]]gang zhig kho na'i gsung ni sems can rnams kyi mos pa'i sems kyi dbang gis kun gyi sgra/_/gzhan gyi rna bar son pas dam pa'i lam ni yang dag ston par mdzad pa de la 'dud/_/rnam pa kun gyi mchog ldan pa'i/_/sku ni mos pa sna tshogs las/_/rang rang bsam pas 
[\[200a.3\]]sems can gyis/_/mthong ba sprul pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/gang zhig sems can kun gyi sgras/_/rdzu 'phrul bdag nyid rab gsal byed/_/sems can bsam pa'i dbang gis 'di/_/longs spyod rdzogs sku'i mtshan nyid do/_/mi rtag pa min rtag pa'ang min/_/gcig min 
[\[200a.4\]]du ma'i mtshan nyid min/_/dngos min dngos po med pa'ang min/_/'di ni rten bral chos sku'o/_/stong nyid snying rje dbyer med cing /_/chags dang chags bral rnam par spangs/_/shes rab ma yin thabs kyang min/_/'di ni ngo bo nyid sku gzhan/_/dus kyi 'khor lo zhes 
[\[200a.5\]]grags pa/_/sku bzhi'i bdag nyid zhi ba la/_/dngos po kun gyis phyag 'tshal te/_/sgrub thabs le'u'i rgya cher 'grel/_/'jam pa'i dpal gyis bskul gyur pas/_/'jig rten dbang po chu skyes 'dzin/_/sprul pa'i skur ni gyur pa bdag_/pad ma dkar pos bri bar 
[\[200a.6\]]bya/_/'dir dpal ldan ka lA pa'i grong gi lho/_ma la ya'i skyed mos tshal gyi dpal dus kyi 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor gyi khang pa'i shar sgo'i mthar rin po che'i sgo khyams na seng ge'i khri la bzhugs pa bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gyi sprul pa'i sku mi'i dbang po grags pa 
[\[200a.7\]]la nyi ma'i shing rtas gsol ba btab par gyur pas mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las sgrub thabs kyi le'ur zla ba bzang pos gsol ba btab pa dang /_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi lan gyi gsung tshigs su bcad pa dang pos 'khor chen po la rab tu bstan par gyur 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]pa rgyal ba skyod mdzad bdag gis dbang bskur bdun po rnams dang bum pa dang ni gsang ba'i dbang bskur dang /_/shes rab ye shes dbang bskur srid pa'i 'jigs pa 'joms pa rnal 'byor rnams kyis bgrod bya bzhi pa thob/_/slar yang rgyal ba mchog dang bcas pa sna tshogs 
[\[200b.2\]]gtso bo'i sgrub thabs yang dag par ni bdag zhu'o/_/zla ba bzang po'i tshig gsan nas ni rgyal ba'i bdag pos rdo rje can dag gi yang sgrub thabs gsungs zhes pa ste 'dir tshigs su bcad pa'i rkang pa gsum gyis sgrub thabs kyi le'u bstan pa'i slad du zla ba bzang pos gsol ba 
[\[200b.3\]]btab pa'o/_/de nas rkang pa bzhi pa nas brtsams te ji srid le'u yongs su rdzogs pa de srid ni bcom ldan 'das kyi lan gyi gsung ngo /_/da ni zla ba bzang po'i tshig gsan nas ni rgyal ba'i bdag po bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa dus kyi 'khor lo'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 
[\[200b.4\]]zhugs pas rdo rje can bcom ldan 'das dpal dus kyi 'khor lo'i sgrub thabs gsungs te yang yig gis mi bskyod pa la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang rdo rje dbyings kyi dbang phyug ma la sogs pa lha mo rnams dang phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[200b.5\]]rnams dang sgra rdo rje ma la sogs pa yul gyi lha mo rnams dang gtsug tor la sogs pa khro bo'i rgyal po chen po rnams dang shin tu sngon mo la sogs pa khro bo'i lha mo rnams dang tsaR+tsi kA la sogs pa ma mo rnams dang khyab 'jug la sogs pa lha rnams dang rgyal ba la sogs pa klu'i 
[\[200b.6\]]rgyal po rnams dang khyi gdong ma la sogs pa rab gtum ma rnams dang gzhan rnams kyi yang so so'i sgrub thabs rgyal ba'i bdag pos gsungs te gzugs kyi sgom pas 'og min gyi srid pa'i mthar thug pa 'jig rten pa'i dngos grub bsgrub pa'i ched du'o zhes pa ni bcom ldan 
[\[200b.7\]]'das kyi nges pa'o/_/da ni dus rnam par dag pas bcom ldan 'das kyi gzugs brtags pa mdor bstan pa gsungs pa zla ba'i sku zhes pa la sogs pa ste 'dir dang po'i sangs rgyas las bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa/_nyin mo nyi ma rdul rdo rje/_/bsam pa'i dbye

[\[201a.1\]]bas mtshan mo zla/_khu ba pad+ma de dag gcig_/dus kyi 'khor lo bde chen po/_/zhes pa dang /_de bzhin du rgyud gzhan dang gzhan dag las kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis spyir gsungs pa/_nyin mo bcom ldan rdo rje can/_/mtshan mo shes rab rab tu grags/_/ji ltar

[\[201a.4\]]yin no zhes pa'o/_/de ltar dus kyi 'khor lo 'di'i sgrub thabs ni skye ba dang 'jig pa rnam par nyams pa'i don du rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis 'chad par 'gyur ba'i rim pas bya'o/_/gzugs brtags pa'i nges pa ni zla ba'i sku zhes pa la sogs pa ste dus sbyor bcu gnyis kyi

[\[204a.1\]]pa dang shes rab kyi chos 'byung gru gsum mo/_/phyi rol du mi yul la rdo rje'i rang bzhin rtsibs bcu zlum po ste nang du ma'i lus so/_/de la phyi rol du ya~M yig yongs su gyur pa las rlung gi dkyil 'khor nag po dbus su gzhu'i rnam pa thad kar dpag tshad 'bum phrag

[\[258a.4\]]par shes pa'i mtshams kyi dbang gis lha'i mig gis mthong ngo /_/de nas chags pa dang bral ba'i mtshams kyi dbang gis sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis gzigs so/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa'i mtshams kyi dbang gis shes rab kyi spyan gyis gzigs so/_/
[\[258a.5\]]de nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi mtshams kyi thugs kyi dbang gis ye shes kyi spyan gyis gzigs te lhag ma kun las rnam grol zhing zhes so/_/de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sha la sogs pa'i spyan lnga po rnams ni stong pa nyid gzigs pa'i slad du 
[\[258a.6\]]ste/_sems can gzhan rnams ni stong pa nyid lta ba'i yul la dmus long ngo /_/'di rnams kyi rgyas pa ni mchog tu mi 'gyur ba'i ye shes grub pa las brjod par bya ste de kho na nyid bsgom pa'i nges pa'o/_/de kho na nyid ma yin pa'i sgrub pa la mngon sum min 
[\[258a.7\]]na rjes su dpag pa shi ba'i lus bzhin de nyid min pa yis ni brtags pa gang /_de ni ri mo sogs la blta bya zhes pa ni sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sku gzugs ri mor byas pa dang lugs ma'am dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo bris nas nges pa'i rnam par 
[\[258b\]]
[\[258b.1\]]blta bar bya ste byis pa'i rnal 'byor pa dman pa rnams kyi bsgom pa'i don du'o zhes pa rnam par rtog pa'i sgom pa'i nges pa'o/_/da ni rnam par rtog pa'i sgom pa'i thabs gsungs pa gzugs zhes pa la sogs pa ste 'dir byis pa'i rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi rang gi sems 
[\[258b.2\]]kyi nus pas gzugs zhes pa lha gcig gam dkyil 'khor bris skur bris pa bsgom par bya ste dang por bris sku la ni de nyid min pas rang sems bsgom par bya zhes so/_/de kho na nyid kyis kyang nam mkha' la ni de nyid sbyor ba dag gis zhes pa stong pa nyid dang snying rje'i 
[\[258b.3\]]sbyor bas rang sems ma tshang med pa mtha' dag mthong gyur pa zhes pa rnam pa thams cad pa ste gzugs dang dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lor brtags pa med pa'i phyir ro/_/de ltar gzugs la sogs pa bris skur bris pa brtags pa'am stong pa nyid kyi gzugs ma 
[\[258b.4\]]brtags pa lo yi phyed dam lo gcig bla ma'i nges pa'i dbang gis ji srid nyid du rang sems brtan par gyur gyi bar du'o/_/de nas phyag rgya'i sbyor bas zhes pa ni 'dir so sor sdud pa dang bsam gtan dang srog rtsol dang 'dzin pa'i stobs kyis rang sems brtan pa skyes par 
[\[258b.5\]]gyur pa na de nas phyag rgya'i sbyor bas nyin zhag 'ga' zhig rnams kyis zhes pa ni dus kyi 'khor lo'i nyin zhag nyi shu rtsa lnga lhag pa'i brgya phrag bcu gcig gis so zhes pa nges pa ste nyin zhag 'di rnams kyis byang chub kyi sems 'gyur ba med pa nyid du rab tu 'gyur te dri ma med 
[\[258b.6\]]pa nyid du 'gyur ro zhes 'brel lo/_/'dir bris sku dang sku gzugs dang glegs bam bri ba'i slad du nye gnas chos smra ba btsal bar bya ste des bris sku dang sku gzugs dang glegs bam la sogs pa bya ste drag po dang zhi ba'i bya bas so/_/sngar gsungs pa'i mchod yon 
[\[258b.7\]]la sogs pa phul nas slob dpon la mchod pa bya'o/_/dge 'dun la mchod ston dang tshogs kyi 'khor lo dbul bar bya'o/_/rab gnas kyi dus kyi cho ga ji lta ba de bzhin du mchod yon 'bul ba'i dus su yang ji ltar nus pas bya'o zhes pa ni nges pa'o/_/da ni 
[\[259a\]]
[\[259a.1\]]rdo rje'i tshig gi nges pa gsungs pa rgyud kyi rgyal po zhes pa la sogs pa ste 'dir rgyud kyi rgyal po thams cad las ni zhes pa 'dus pa la sogs pa thabs kyi rgyud dang 'khor lo sdom pa la sogs pa shes rab kyi rgyud las nges par rdo rje'i tshig sngar gsungs 
[\[259a.2\]]pa'i mi 'gyur ba'i bde ba de bcom ldan 'das kyis rnal 'byor pa rnams dag la gsungs te/_de bzhin du gsungs pa/_'di ltar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/_/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas a las byung /_/a ni yig 'bru kun gyi mchog_/don chen 
[\[259a.3\]]'gyur med dam pa yin/_/srog chen po ni skye ba med/_/tshig tu brjod pa spangs pa ste/_/brjod pa kun gyi rgyu yi mchog_/tshig kun rab tu gsal bar byed/_/ces pa la sogs pa nas ye shes sku nyid khyod la 'dud ces pa'i mthar thug pa tshigs su 
[\[259a.4\]]bcad pa drug cu rtsa gnyis lhag pa'i brgyas mtshan yang dag par brjod pa las ye shes kyi sku gsungs pa de nyid rdo rje'i tshig tu gsungs te de nyid ni rgyud kyi rgyal po thams cad du byis pa rnams ni smin par byed pa'i don du mchog gi thugs rje dag gis sna tshogs gtso bos 
[\[259a.5\]]sbas te byis pa rnams la dbang po gnyis kyi bde ba rab tu bstan to/_/de'i phyir rdo rje'i tshig de ni dbye ba byas nas nyin zhag so so'i dus su ste nyin dang mtshan mor rnal 'byor pa yis sngar gsungs pa'i mig gis bsgom par bya ste phyag rgya'i dngos grub don slad ces pa 
[\[259a.6\]]phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub kyi slad du'o/_/de ci zhig ce na de ni rgyal ba mchog gi skyed mdzad gzhom du med pa dus kyi 'khor lo'o zhes pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nges pa ste rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi gnas thob pa'i slad du rgyud gzhan thams cad las rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis rtogs par bya'o/_/
[\[259a.7\]]bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo'i rgyas 'grel rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dri ma med pa'i 'od las sna tshogs pa'i sgrub thabs kyi mdor bsdus chen po ste lnga pa'o//_//sgrub thabs kyi le'u yi rgyas 'grel rdzogs so//_//
